### Algebraic Expressions: Prime Factorisation and Factorisation

#### 1. Prime Factorisation using Difference of Squares
This method uses the identity $x^2 - y^2 = (x - y)(x + y)$ to break down numbers into their prime factors.

**Example d: Factorise 99**
$$99 = 100 - 1$$
$$99 = (10 - 1)(10 + 1)$$
$$99 = 9 \times 11$$
$$99 = 3^2 \times 11$$

**Example e: Factorise 1599**
$$1599 = 1600 - 1$$
$$1599 = (40 - 1)(40 + 1)$$
$$1599 = 39 \times 41$$
$$1599 = 3 \times 13 \times 41$$

---

#### 2. Factorising Algebraic Expressions
Factorisation is the process of writing an expression as a product of its factors.

**Exercise 29: Factorise the following expressions**

**a) $a^2 - 9$**
$$a^2 - 9 = (a - 3)(a + 3)$$

**b) $9b^2 - 81$**
$$9b^2 - 81 = 9(b^2 - 9)$$
$$9b^2 - 81 = 9(b - 3)(b + 3)$$

**c) $m^2 - \frac{1}{9}$**
$$m^2 - \frac{1}{9} = (m - \frac{1}{3})(m + \frac{1}{3})$$

**d) $5 - 5a^2b^6$**
$$5 - 5a^2b^6 = 5(1 - a^2b^6)$$
$$5 - 5a^2b^6 = 5(1 - ab^3)(1 + ab^3)$$

**e) $16ba^4 - 81b$**
$$16ba^4 - 81b = b(16a^4 - 81)$$
$$16ba^4 - 81b = b(4a^2 - 9)(4a^2 + 9)$$
$$16ba^4 - 81b = b(2a - 3)(2a + 3)(4a^2 + 9)$$

**f) $a^2 - 10a + 25$**
This is a perfect square trinomial:
$$a^2 - 10a + 25 = (a - 5)(a - 5)$$
$$a^2 - 10a + 25 = (a - 5)^2$$

**g) $16b^2 + 56b + 49$**
This is a perfect square trinomial:
$$16b^2 + 56b + 49 = (4b + 7)(4b + 7)$$
$$16b^2 + 56b + 49 = (4b + 7)^2$$

---

### Algebraic Factorisation: Worked Examples

This section provides step-by-step solutions for various algebraic factorisation problems, covering common factors, difference of squares, trinomials, and grouping.

---

#### h) Factorise: $-4b^2 - 144b^8 + 48b^5$
**Solution:**
1. Extract the common factor $-4b^2$:
   $$-4b^2 - 144b^8 + 48b^5 = -4b^2(1 + 36b^6 - 12b^3)$$
2. Rearrange and factor the perfect square trinomial:
   $$= -4b^2(6b^3 - 1)(6b^3 - 1)$$
   $$= -4b^2(6b^3 - 1)^2$$

---

#### i) Factorise: $16 - x^4$
**Solution:**
1. Use the difference of squares formula $a^2 - b^2 = (a - b)(a + b)$:
   $$16 - x^4 = (4 - x^2)(4 + x^2)$$
2. Factor the difference of squares $(4 - x^2)$ further:
   $$= (2 - x)(2 + x)(4 + x^2)$$

---

#### j) Factorise: $7x^2 - 14x + 7xy - 14y$
**Solution:**
1. Extract the common factor $7$:
   $$7x^2 - 14x + 7xy - 14y = 7(x^2 - 2x + xy - 2y)$$
2. Factor by grouping:
   $$= 7[x(x - 2) + y(x - 2)]$$
   $$= 7(x - 2)(x + y)$$

---

#### k) Factorise: $y^2 - 7y - 30$
**Solution:**
1. Find two numbers that multiply to $-30$ and add to $-7$:
   $$y^2 - 7y - 30 = (y - 10)(y + 3)$$

---

#### l) Factorise: $1 - x - x^2 + x^3$
**Solution:**
1. Group terms:
   $$1 - x - x^2 + x^3 = (1 - x) - x^2(1 - x)$$
2. Factor out the common binomial $(1 - x)$:
   $$= (1 - x)(1 - x^2)$$
3. Factor the difference of squares $(1 - x^2)$:
   $$= (1 - x)(1 - x)(1 + x)$$
   $$= (1 - x)^2(1 + x)$$

---

#### m) Factorise: $-3(1 - p^2) + p + 1$
**Solution:**
1. Expand the difference of squares $(1 - p^2)$:
   $$-3(1 - p)(1 + p) + (1 + p)$$
2. Factor out the common binomial $(1 + p)$:
   $$= (1 + p)[-3(1 - p) + 1]$$
   $$= (1 + p)(-3 + 3p + 1)$$
   $$= (1 + p)(3p - 2)$$

---

#### n) Factorise: $x^2 - 2x + 1 - y^4$
**Solution:**
1. Recognise the perfect square trinomial $(x^2 - 2x + 1)$:
   $$x^2 - 2x + 1 - y^4 = (x - 1)^2 - (y^2)^2$$
2. Use the difference of squares formula:
   $$= (x - 1 - y^2)(x - 1 + y^2)$$

---

#### o) Factorise: $4b(x^3 - 1) + x(1 - x^3)$
**Solution:**
1. Rewrite $(1 - x^3)$ as $-(x^3 - 1)$ to create a common factor:
   $$4b(x^3 - 1) - x(x^3 - 1)$$
2. Factor out $(x^3 - 1)$:
   $$= (x^3 - 1)(4b - x)$$
3. Apply the difference of cubes formula $a^3 - b^3 = (a - b)(a^2 + ab + b^2)$:
   $$= (x - 1)(x^2 + x + 1)(4b - x)$$

---

### Algebraic Expressions: Factorisation Exercises

This section covers advanced factorisation techniques, including common factors, difference of cubes, sum of cubes, and grouping.

---

#### p) Factorising by grouping
**Problem:** $3m(v - 7) + 19(-7 + v)$
**Solution:**
$$3m(v - 7) + 19(-7 + v) = 3m(v - 7) + 19(v - 7)$$
$$= (v - 7)(3m + 19)$$

---

#### q) Factorising by grouping
**Problem:** $3f(z + 3) + 19(3 + z)$
**Solution:**
$$3f(z + 3) + 19(3 + z) = 3f(z + 3) + 19(z + 3)$$
$$= (3f + 19)(z + 3)$$

---

#### r) Difference of cubes
**Problem:** $3p^3 - \frac{1}{9}$
**Solution:**
$$3p^3 - \frac{1}{9} = 3(p^3 - \frac{1}{27})$$
$$= 3(p - \frac{1}{3})(p^2 + \frac{p}{3} + \frac{1}{9})$$

---

#### s) Difference of cubes
**Problem:** $8x^6 - 125y^9$
**Solution:**
$$8x^6 - 125y^9 = (2x^2 - 5y^3)(4x^4 + 10x^2y^3 + 25y^6)$$

---

#### t) Difference of cubes with binomials
**Problem:** $(2 + p)^3 - 8(p + 1)^3$
**Solution:**
$$(2 + p)^3 - 8(p + 1)^3 = [(p + 2) - 2(p + 1)][(p + 2)^2 + 2(p + 2)(p + 1) + 4(p + 1)^2]$$
$$= [p + 2 - 2p - 2][p^2 + 4p + 4 + 2p^2 + 6p + 4 + 4p^2 + 8p + 4]$$
$$= (-p)(12 + 18p + 7p^2)$$

---

#### u) Factorising by grouping
**Problem:** $\frac{1}{3}a^3 - a^2b + 2a^2b - 6ab^2 + 3ab^2 - 9b^3$
**Solution:**
$$\frac{1}{3}a^3 - a^2b + 2a^2b - 6ab^2 + 3ab^2 - 9b^3 = \frac{1}{3}a^2(a - 3b) + 2ab(a - 3b) + 3b^2(a - 3b)$$
$$= (\frac{1}{3}a^2 + 2ab + 3b^2)(a - 3b)$$
$$= \frac{(a^2 + 6ab + 9b^2)(a - 3b)}{3}$$
$$= \frac{(a + 3b)^2(a - 3b)}{3}$$

---

#### v) Trinomial factorisation
**Problem:** $6a^2 - 17a + 5$
**Solution:**
$$6a^2 - 17a + 5 = (2a - 5)(3a - 1)$$

---

#### w) Trinomial factorisation
**Problem:** $s^2 + 2s - 15$
**Solution:**
$$s^2 + 2s - 15 = (s - 3)(s + 5)$$

---

#### x) Factorising by grouping
**Problem:** $16v + 24h + 2j^5v + 3j^5h$
**Solution:**
$$16v + 24h + 2j^5v + 3j^5h = 8(2v + 3h) + j^5(2v + 3h)$$
$$= (2v + 3h)(8 + j^5)$$

---

### Algebraic Factorisation

This section covers advanced factorisation techniques, including grouping, trinomials, and the difference/sum of cubes.

---

#### 1. Factorisation by Grouping
When an expression has four terms, we group them into pairs to find a common binomial factor.

**Example y:**
$$18h - 45g + 2m^3h - 5m^3g$$
**Solution:**
$$18h - 45g + 2m^3h - 5m^3g = 9(2h - 5g) + m^3(2h - 5g)$$
$$= (2h - 5g)(9 + m^3)$$

**Example z:**
$$63d - 18s + 7u^2d - 2u^2s$$
**Solution:**
$$63d - 18s + 7u^2d - 2u^2s = 9(7d - 2s) + u^2(7d - 2s)$$
$$= (7d - 2s)(9 + u^2)$$

---

#### 2. Factorising Trinomials and Special Products
This section covers quadratic trinomials, difference of cubes, and sum of cubes.

**30. Factorise the following:**

**a) $6a^2 + 14a + 8$**
**Solution:**
$$6a^2 + 14a + 8 = 2(3a^2 + 7a + 4)$$
$$= 2(a + 1)(3a + 4)$$

**b) $6g^2 - 15g - 9$**
**Solution:**
$$6g^2 - 15g - 9 = 3(2g^2 - 5g - 3)$$
$$= 3(g - 3)(2g + 1)$$

**c) $125g^3 - r^3$ (Difference of Cubes)**
**Solution:**
$$125g^3 - r^3 = (5g - r)(25g^2 + 5gr + r^2)$$

**d) $8r^3 + z^3$ (Sum of Cubes)**
**Solution:**
$$8r^3 + z^3 = (2r + z)(4r^2 - 2rz + z^2)$$

**e) $14m - 4n + 7jm - 2jn$**
**Solution:**
$$14m - 4n + 7jm - 2jn = 2(7m - 2n) + j(7m - 2n)$$
$$= (7m - 2n)(2 + j)$$

**f) $25d - 15m + 5yd - 3ym$**
**Solution:**
$$25d - 15m + 5yd - 3ym = 5(5d - 3m) + y(5d - 3m)$$
$$= (5d - 3m)(5 + y)$$

**g) $g^3 - 27$ (Difference of Cubes)**
**Solution:**
$$g^3 - 27 = (g - 3)(g^2 + 3g + 9)$$

**h) $z^3 + 125$ (Sum of Cubes)**
**Solution:**
$$z^3 + 125 = (z + 5)(z^2 - 5z + 25)$$

---

### Algebraic Factorization Exercises

This section covers advanced factorization techniques, including the difference of two squares, common factors, and trinomial factorization.

---

#### i) Factorize $b^2 - (3a - 2b)^2$
**Solution:**
Using the difference of two squares $x^2 - y^2 = (x - y)(x + y)$:
$$b^2 - (3a - 2b)^2 = (b - (3a - 2b))(b + 3a - 2b)$$
$$= (b - 3a + 2b)(3a - b)$$
$$= (3b - 3a)(3a - b)$$
$$= 3(b - a)(3a - b)$$

---

#### j) Factorize $9y^2 - (4x + 2y)^2$
**Solution:**
$$9y^2 - (4x + 2y)^2 = (3y + (4x + 2y))(3y - (4x + 2y))$$
$$= (3y + 4x + 2y)(3y - 4x - 2y)$$
$$= (4x + 5y)(y - 4x)$$

---

#### k) Factorize $16x^6 - 3y^8$
**Solution:**
Factor out the common factor $4$:
$$16x^6 - 3y^8 = 4(4x^6 - \frac{3}{4}y^8)$$
*(Note: Based on the provided text, the solution follows the logic of difference of squares)*:
$$= 4(2x^3 - \sqrt{3}y^4)(2x^3 + \sqrt{3}y^4)$$

---

#### l) Factorize $\frac{1}{6}a^2 - 24b^4$
**Solution:**
Factor out the common fraction $\frac{1}{6}$:
$$\frac{1}{6}a^2 - 24b^4 = \frac{1}{6}(a^2 - 144b^4)$$
$$= \frac{1}{6}(a - 12b^2)(a + 12b^2)$$

---

#### m) Factorize $4(a - 3) - 81x^2(a - 3)$
**Solution:**
Factor out the common binomial $(a - 3)$:
$$4(a - 3) - 81x^2(a - 3) = (a - 3)(4 - 81x^2)$$
Using difference of squares on $(4 - 81x^2)$:
$$= (a - 3)(2 - 9x)(2 + 9x)$$

---

#### n) Factorize $(2 + b)^2 - 11(2 + b) - 12$
**Solution:**
Let $k = (2 + b)$. The expression becomes $k^2 - 11k - 12$:
$$(k - 12)(k + 1)$$
Substitute back $(2 + b)$:
$$((2 + b) - 12)((2 + b) + 1)$$
$$= (b - 10)(b + 3)$$

---

#### o) Factorize $2x^2 + 7xy + 5y^2$
**Solution:**
Using trinomial factorization:
$$2x^2 + 7xy + 5y^2 = (2x + 5y)(x + y)$$

---

#### p) Factorize $x^2 - 2xy - 15y^2$
**Solution:**
Find factors of $-15$ that add to $-2$:
$$x^2 - 2xy - 15y^2 = (x - 5y)(x + 3y)$$

---

#### q) Factorize $4x^4 + 11x^2 + 6$
**Solution:**
Treat as a quadratic in terms of $x^2$:
$$4x^4 + 11x^2 + 6 = (4x^2 + 3)(x^2 + 2)$$

---

### Algebraic Factorization and Simplification Exercises

This section covers advanced factorization techniques, including trinomials, grouping, difference of cubes, sum of cubes, and algebraic simplification.

---

#### Factorization Exercises

**r) Factorize $6x^4 - 38x^2 + 40$**
$$6x^4 - 38x^2 + 40 = 2(3x^4 - 19x^2 + 20)$$
$$= 2(3x^2 - 4)(x^2 - 5)$$

**s) Factorize $9a^2x + 9a^2y + 27a^2 - b^2x - b^2y - 3b^2$**
$$9a^2x + 9a^2y + 27a^2 - b^2x - b^2y - 3b^2 = (9a^2 - b^2)(x + y + 3)$$
$$= (3a + b)(3a - b)(x + y + 3)$$

**t) Factorize $2(2y^2 - 5y) - 24$**
$$2(2y^2 - 5y) - 24 = 2(2y^2 - 5y) - 2(12)$$
$$= 2(2y^2 - 5y - 12)$$
$$= 2(2y + 3)(y - 4)$$

**u) Factorize $\frac{1}{2}x^3 - \frac{9}{2}x - 2x^2 + 18$**
$$\frac{1}{2}x^3 - \frac{9}{2}x - 2x^2 + 18 = \frac{x^3 - 9x - 4x^2 + 36}{2}$$
$$= \frac{x^2(x - 4) - 9(x - 4)}{2}$$
$$= \frac{(x - 4)(x^2 - 9)}{2}$$
$$= \frac{(x - 4)(x - 3)(x + 3)}{2}$$

**v) Factorize $27r^3s^3 - 1$ (Difference of Cubes)**
$$27r^3s^3 - 1 = (3rs - 1)(9r^2s^2 + 3rs + 1)$$

**w) Factorize $\frac{1}{125h^3} + r^3$ (Sum of Cubes)**
$$\frac{1}{125h^3} + r^3 = \left(\frac{1}{5h} + r\right)\left(\frac{1}{25h^2} - \frac{r}{5h} + r^2\right)$$

**x) Factorize $j(64n^3 - b^3) + k(64n^3 - b^3)$**
$$j(64n^3 - b^3) + k(64n^3 - b^3) = (j + k)(64n^3 - b^3)$$
$$= (j + k)(4n - b)(16n^2 + 4nb + b^2)$$

$$(a - 2)^2 - a(a + 4) = a^2 - 4a + 4 - a^2 - 4a$$
$$= -8a + 4$$

### Algebraic Expressions: Worked Examples

#### b) Expansion of a Binomial and Trinomial
**Problem:** Expand $(5a - 4b)(25a^2 + 20ab + 16b^2)$

**Solution:**
$$(5a - 4b)(25a^2 + 20ab + 16b^2) = 125a^3 + 100a^2b + 80ab^2 - 100a^2b - 80ab^2 - 64b^3$$
$$= 125a^3 - 64b^3$$

---

#### c) Expansion of Three Factors
**Problem:** Expand $(2m - 3)(4m^2 + 9)(2m + 3)$

**Solution:**
$$(2m - 3)(4m^2 + 9)(2m + 3) = (4m^2 - 9)(4m^2 + 9)$$
$$= 16m^4 - 81$$

---

#### d) Expansion of Trinomials
**Problem:** Expand $(a + 2b - c)(a + 2b + c)$

**Solution:**
$$(a + 2b - c)(a + 2b + c) = a^2 + 2ab + ac + 2ab + 4b^2 + 2bc - ac - 2bc - c^2$$
$$= a^2 + 4ab + 4b^2 - c^2$$

---

#### e) Division of Algebraic Fractions
**Problem:** Simplify $\frac{m^2 + 11m + 18}{4(m^2 - 4)} \div \frac{3m^2 + 27m}{24m^2 - 48m}$

**Solution:**
$$\frac{m^2 + 11m + 18}{4(m^2 - 4)} \div \frac{3m^2 + 27m}{24m^2 - 48m} = \frac{m^2 + 11m + 18}{4(m^2 - 4)} \times \frac{24m^2 - 48m}{3m^2 + 27m}$$
$$= \frac{(m + 9)(m + 2)}{4(m - 2)(m + 2)} \times \frac{24m(m - 2)}{3m(m + 9)}$$
$$= \frac{1}{4} \times \frac{24}{3}$$
$$= 2$$

---

#### f) Division of Algebraic Fractions
**Problem:** Simplify $\frac{t^2 + 9t + 18}{5(t^2 - 9)} \div \frac{4t^2 + 24t}{100t^2 - 300t}$

**Solution:**
$$\frac{t^2 + 9t + 18}{5(t^2 - 9)} \div \frac{4t^2 + 24t}{100t^2 - 300t} = \frac{t^2 + 9t + 18}{5(t^2 - 9)} \times \frac{100t^2 - 300t}{4t^2 + 24t}$$
$$= \frac{(t + 6)(t + 3)}{5(t - 3)(t + 3)} \times \frac{100t(t - 3)}{4t(t + 6)}$$
$$= \frac{1}{5} \times \frac{100}{4}$$
$$= 5$$

---

#### g) Simplifying Algebraic Fractions
**Problem:** Simplify $\frac{4 - b^2}{3b - 6}$

**Solution:**
$$\frac{4 - b^2}{3b - 6} = \frac{(2 - b)(2 + b)}{3(b - 2)}$$
$$= -\frac{2 + b}{3}$$

---

#### h) Simplifying Algebraic Fractions
**Problem:** Simplify $\frac{x^2 + 2x + 4}{x^3 - 8}$

**Solution:**
*(Note: This expression requires the difference of cubes factorization: $x^3 - 8 = (x - 2)(x^2 + 2x + 4)$)*

$$\frac{x^2 + 2x + 4}{(x - 2)(x^2 + 2x + 4)} = \frac{1}{x - 2}$$

---

### Algebraic Expressions: Simplifying Rational Expressions

This section covers the simplification of complex algebraic fractions involving multiplication and division. The key strategy is to factorize all numerators and denominators completely before cancelling common factors.

---

#### Worked Examples

**i) Simplify:** $\frac{x^2 - 5x - 14}{3x + 6}$

**Solution:**
1. Factorize the numerator: $x^2 - 5x - 14 = (x - 7)(x + 2)$
2. Factorize the denominator: $3x + 6 = 3(x + 2)$
3. Simplify:
$$\frac{(x - 7)(x + 2)}{3(x + 2)} = \frac{x - 7}{3}$$

---

**j) Simplify:** $\frac{d^2 + 23d + 132}{5(d^2 - 121)} \div \frac{4d^2 + 48d}{100d^2 - 1100d}$

**Solution:**
1. Convert division to multiplication by the reciprocal:
$$\frac{d^2 + 23d + 132}{5(d^2 - 121)} \times \frac{100d^2 - 1100d}{4d^2 + 48d}$$
2. Factorize all terms:
$$= \frac{(d + 12)(d + 11)}{5(d - 11)(d + 11)} \times \frac{100d(d - 11)}{4d(d + 12)}$$
3. Cancel common factors:
$$= \frac{1}{5} \times \frac{100}{4} = \frac{1}{5} \times 25 = 5$$

---

**k) Simplify:** $\frac{a - 2}{a^2 + 4a + 3} \div \frac{(a - 1)(a + 1)}{a - 1} \times \frac{a^2 - 2a - 15}{a - 2}$

**Solution:**
1. Convert division to multiplication:
$$= \frac{a - 2}{(a + 1)(a + 3)} \times \frac{a - 1}{(a - 1)(a + 1)} \times \frac{(a + 3)(a - 5)}{a - 2}$$
2. Cancel common factors:
$$= \frac{a - 5}{(a + 2)^2}$$

---

**l) Simplify:** $\frac{a + 6}{a^2 + 12a + 11} \times \frac{a^2 + 14a + 33}{a + 3} \div \frac{a^3 + 216}{a + 1}$

**Solution:**
1. Factorize and convert division to multiplication:
$$= \frac{a + 6}{(a + 11)(a + 1)} \times \frac{(a + 11)(a + 3)}{a + 3} \times \frac{a + 1}{(a + 6)(a^2 - 6a + 36)}$$
*(Note: Sum of cubes $a^3 + 216 = (a + 6)(a^2 - 6a + 36)$)*
2. Cancel common factors:
$$= \frac{1}{a^2 - 6a + 36}$$

---

**m) Simplify:** $2 \div \frac{a + b}{a + 2b} \times \frac{b^2 - ba - 6a^2}{a^2 - 4b^2} \times \frac{a^2 - b - 2b^2}{3a - b}$

**Solution:**
1. Factorize and convert division to multiplication:
$$= 2 \times \frac{a + 2b}{a + b} \times \frac{(b - 3a)(b + 2a)}{(a - 2b)(a + 2b)} \times \frac{(a - 2b)(a + b)}{3a - b}$$
2. Simplify by cancelling terms (noting that $(b - 3a) = -(3a - b)$):
$$= 2 \times \frac{-(3a - b)(b + 2a)}{1} \times \frac{1}{3a - b}$$
$$= -2(b + 2a)$$

---

### Algebraic Expressions: Worked Examples

This section covers the simplification of algebraic fractions, including factoring by grouping, division of fractions, and finding common denominators for addition and subtraction.

---

#### n) Simplify: $\frac{st + sb + 31t + 31b}{t + b}$

**Solution:**
Factor the numerator by grouping:
$$\frac{st + sb + 31t + 31b}{t + b} = \frac{s(t + b) + 31(t + b)}{(t + b)}$$
Factor out the common binomial $(t + b)$:
$$= \frac{(t + b)(s + 31)}{(t + b)}$$
Cancel the common term $(t + b)$:
$$= s + 31$$

---

#### o) Simplify: $\frac{ny + nq + 8y + 8q}{y + q}$

**Solution:**
Factor the numerator by grouping:
$$\frac{ny + nq + 8y + 8q}{y + q} = \frac{n(y + q) + 8(y + q)}{(y + q)}$$
Factor out the common binomial $(y + q)$:
$$= \frac{(y + q)(n + 8)}{(y + q)}$$
Cancel the common term $(y + q)$:
$$= n + 8$$

---

#### p) Simplify: $\frac{p^2 - q^2}{p} \div \frac{p + q}{p^2 - pq}$

**Solution:**
Convert division to multiplication by the reciprocal and factor the expressions:
$$\frac{p^2 - q^2}{p} \times \frac{p^2 - pq}{p + q} = \frac{(p - q)(p + q)}{p} \times \frac{p(p - q)}{p + q}$$
Cancel common terms $(p + q)$ and $p$:
$$= (p - q)(p - q)$$
$$= (p - q)^2$$
Expand the square:
$$= p^2 - 2pq + q^2$$

---

#### q) Simplify: $\frac{2}{x} + \frac{x}{2} - \frac{2x}{3}$

**Solution:**
Find the lowest common denominator (LCD), which is $6x$:
$$\frac{2}{x} + \frac{x}{2} - \frac{2x}{3} = \frac{2(6) + x(3x) - 2x(2x)}{6x}$$
Simplify the numerator:
$$= \frac{12 + 3x^2 - 4x^2}{6x}$$
$$= \frac{12 - x^2}{6x}$$

---

#### r) Simplify: $\frac{1}{a + 7} - \frac{a + 7}{a^2 - 49}$

**Solution:**
Factor the denominator $a^2 - 49$ as a difference of two squares $(a + 7)(a - 7)$:
$$\frac{1}{a + 7} - \frac{a + 7}{(a + 7)(a - 7)}$$
Simplify the second fraction by canceling $(a + 7)$:
$$= \frac{1}{a + 7} - \frac{1}{a - 7}$$
Find the common denominator $(a + 7)(a - 7)$:
$$= \frac{(a - 7) - (a + 7)}{(a + 7)(a - 7)}$$
$$= \frac{a - 7 - a - 7}{(a + 7)(a - 7)}$$
$$= \frac{-14}{(a + 7)(a - 7)}$$

---

#### s) Simplify: $\frac{x + 2}{2x^3} + 16$

**Solution:**
Express $16$ as a fraction with denominator $2x^3$:
$$\frac{x + 2}{2x^3} + \frac{16(2x^3)}{2x^3}$$
Combine the numerators:
$$= \frac{x + 2 + 32x^3}{2x^3}$$
Rearrange terms:
$$= \frac{32x^3 + x + 2}{2x^3}$$

---

### Algebraic Expressions: Operations with Rational Expressions

This section covers the simplification of complex algebraic fractions involving addition, subtraction, and multiplication.

---

#### t) Subtraction of Rational Expressions
**Problem:** Simplify $\frac{1 - 2a}{4a^2 - 1} - \frac{a - 1}{2a^2 - 3a + 1} - \frac{1}{1 - a}$

**Solution:**
1. Factorize the denominators:
   $\frac{1 - 2a}{(2a - 1)(2a + 1)} - \frac{a - 1}{(2a - 1)(a - 1)} - \frac{1}{-(a - 1)}$
2. Simplify terms and adjust signs:
   $= -\frac{(2a - 1)}{(2a - 1)(2a + 1)} - \frac{1}{2a - 1} + \frac{1}{a - 1}$
   $= -\frac{1}{2a + 1} - \frac{1}{2a - 1} + \frac{1}{a - 1}$
3. Combine to find the final expression:
   $= \frac{4a - 1}{(2a + 1)(2a - 1)(a - 1)}$

---

#### u) Addition of Algebraic Fractions
**Problem:** Simplify $\frac{1}{2}x + \frac{x - 2}{3} + 4$

**Solution:**
1. Find a common denominator (6):
   $= \frac{3x + 2(x - 2) + (2)(3)(4)}{6}$
2. Expand and simplify the numerator:
   $= \frac{3x + 2x - 4 + 24}{6}$
   $= \frac{5x + 20}{6}$

---

#### v) Addition of Rational Expressions
**Problem:** Simplify $\frac{1}{x^2 + 2x} + \frac{4x^2 - x - 3}{x^2 + 2x - 3}$

**Solution:**
1. Factorize denominators and numerators:
   $= \frac{1}{x(x + 2)} + \frac{(4x + 3)(x - 1)}{(x - 1)(x + 3)}$
2. Simplify the second fraction:
   $= \frac{1}{x(x + 2)} + \frac{4x + 3}{x + 3}$
3. Find common denominator and expand:
   $= \frac{(x + 3) + x(4x + 3)(x + 2)}{x(x + 2)(x + 3)}$
   $= \frac{x + 3 + x(4x^2 + 11x + 6)}{x(x + 2)(x + 3)}$
   $= \frac{4x^3 + 11x^2 + 7x + 3}{x(x + 2)(x + 3)}$

---

#### w) Addition of Complex Rational Expressions
**Problem:** Simplify $\frac{b^2 + 6b + 9}{b^2 - 9} + \frac{b^2 - 6b + 8}{(b - 2)(b + 3)} + \frac{1}{b + 3}$

**Solution:**
1. Factorize all components:
   $= \frac{(b + 3)^2}{(b + 3)(b - 3)} + \frac{(b - 4)(b - 2)}{(b - 2)(b + 3)} + \frac{1}{b + 3}$
2. Simplify fractions:
   $= \frac{b + 3}{b - 3} + \frac{b - 4}{b + 3} + \frac{1}{b + 3}$
3. Combine over common denominator $(b - 3)(b + 3)$:
   $= \frac{(b + 3)^2 + (b - 3)(b - 4) + (b - 3)}{(b - 3)(b + 3)}$
   $= \frac{b^2 + 6b + 9 + b^2 - 7b + 12 + b - 3}{(b - 3)(b + 3)}$
   $= \frac{2b^2 + 18}{(b - 3)(b + 3)} = \frac{2(b^2 + 9)}{(b - 3)(b + 3)}$

---

#### x) Multiplication of Rational Expressions
**Problem:** Simplify $\frac{x^2 + 2x}{x^2 + x + 6} \times \frac{x^2 + 2x + 1}{x^2 + 3x + 2}$

<!-- DIAGRAM_PLACEHOLDER: diagram -->

---

### Algebraic Expressions: Operations and Simplification

#### 1. Multiplication and Simplification of Rational Expressions
To simplify products of algebraic fractions, factorize the numerators and denominators first, then cancel common factors.

$$\frac{x^2 + 2x}{x^2 + x + 6} \times \frac{x^2 + 2x + 1}{x^2 + 3x + 2} = \frac{x(x + 2)}{x^2 + x + 6} \times \frac{(x + 1)(x + 1)}{(x + 2)(x + 1)}$$
$$= \frac{x(x + 1)}{x^2 + x + 6}$$

---

#### 2. Addition and Subtraction of Algebraic Fractions
To add or subtract fractions, find a common denominator.

**Example y: Addition**
$$\frac{12}{z + 12} + \frac{5}{z - 5}$$
**Solution:**
$$\frac{12(z - 5) + 5(z + 12)}{(z + 12)(z - 5)} = \frac{12z - 60 + 5z + 60}{(z + 12)(z - 5)} = \frac{17z}{(z + 12)(z - 5)}$$

**Example z: Subtraction**
$$\frac{11}{w - 11} - \frac{4}{w - 4}$$
**Solution:**
$$\frac{11(w - 4) - 4(w - 11)}{(w - 11)(w - 4)} = \frac{11w - 44 - 4w + 44}{(w - 11)(w - 4)} = \frac{7w}{(w - 11)(w - 4)}$$

---

#### 3. Proving Algebraic Identities
**Problem 32:** Show that $(2x - 1)^2 - (x - 3)^2 = (x + 2)(3x - 4)$.

**Solution:**
$$(2x - 1)^2 - (x - 3)^2 = (4x^2 - 4x + 1) - (x^2 - 6x + 9)$$
$$= 4x^2 - 4x + 1 - x^2 + 6x - 9$$
$$= 3x^2 + 2x - 8$$
Factorizing the result:
$$= (3x - 4)(x + 2)$$

---

#### 4. Solving for Unknown Terms
**Problem 33:** What must be added to $x^2 - x + 4$ to make it equal to $(x + 2)^2$?

**Solution:**
Let $A$ be the term to be added:
$$(x^2 - x + 4) + A = (x + 2)^2$$
$$A = (x + 2)^2 - (x^2 - x + 4)$$
$$A = (x^2 + 4x + 4) - x^2 + x - 4$$
$$A = 5x$$
Therefore, $5x$ must be added.

---

#### 5. Evaluation of Expressions
**Problem 34:** Evaluate $\frac{x^3 + 1}{x^2 - x + 1}$ if $x = 7,85$ without using a calculator.

**Solution:**
First, simplify the expression using the sum of cubes factorization: $a^3 + b^3 = (a + b)(a^2 - ab + b^2)$.
$$\frac{x^3 + 1}{x^2 - x + 1} = \frac{(x + 1)(x^2 - x + 1)}{x^2 - x + 1}$$
$$= x + 1$$
Substitute $x = 7,85$:
$$7,85 + 1 = 8,85$$

---

### Algebraic Expressions: Worked Examples

#### 35. Multiplication of Algebraic Expressions
**Problem:** With what expression must $(a - 2b)$ be multiplied to get a product of $(a^3 - 8b^3)$?

**Solution:**
Recall the difference of two cubes formula: $x^3 - y^3 = (x - y)(x^2 + xy + y^2)$.
Applying this to $a^3 - 8b^3$ (where $8b^3 = (2b)^3$):
$$(a - 2b)(a^2 + a(2b) + (2b)^2) = a^3 - 8b^3$$
$$(a - 2b)(a^2 + 2ab + 4b^2) = a^3 - 8b^3$$
The required expression is $a^2 + 2ab + 4b^2$.

---

#### 36. Division of Algebraic Expressions
**Problem:** With what expression must $27x^3 + 1$ be divided to get a quotient of $3x + 1$?

**Solution:**
We need to find the value of $\frac{27x^3 + 1}{3x + 1}$.
Using the sum of two cubes formula: $x^3 + y^3 = (x + y)(x^2 - xy + y^2)$.
Here, $27x^3 + 1 = (3x)^3 + (1)^3$:
$$(3x)^3 + 1^3 = (3x + 1)((3x)^2 - (3x)(1) + 1^2)$$
$$27x^3 + 1 = (3x + 1)(9x^2 - 3x + 1)$$
Dividing by $(3x + 1)$:
$$\frac{(3x + 1)(9x^2 - 3x + 1)}{3x + 1} = 9x^2 - 3x + 1$$
The expression is $9x^2 - 3x + 1$.

---

#### 37. Determining Restrictions
Restrictions occur where the denominator of a fraction is equal to zero, as division by zero is undefined.

**a) Problem:** Find the restrictions for $\frac{4}{3x^2 + 2x - 1}$

**Solution:**
Factor the denominator: $3x^2 + 2x - 1 = (3x - 1)(x + 1)$.
The expression is $\frac{4}{(3x - 1)(x + 1)}$.
Set the factors to zero:
$3x - 1 = 0 \implies x = \frac{1}{3}$
$x + 1 = 0 \implies x = -1$
**Restrictions:** $x \neq \frac{1}{3}$ and $x \neq -1$.

**b) Problem:** Find the restrictions for $\frac{a}{3(b - a) + ab - a^2}$

**Solution:**
Simplify the denominator:
$3(b - a) + ab - a^2$
$= 3b - 3a + ab - a^2$
$= 3(b - a) + a(b - a)$
$= (b - a)(3 + a)$
The expression is $\frac{a}{(b - a)(a + 3)}$.
Set the factors to zero:
$b - a = 0 \implies b = a$
$a + 3 = 0 \implies a = -3$
**Restrictions:** $a \neq b$ and $a \neq -3$.

---
